This review examines: 1) mobile CT systems deployed in trucks, trailers, ambulances, and other mobile platforms; 2) telehealth technologies supporting CT-based healthcare; and 3) AI for CT that has received regulatory authorisation or is currently deployed in clinical practice. Applications are evaluated across four clinical functions: screening and diagnosis, patient monitoring, risk prediction, and intervention or therapeutic decision support. The review covers neurological, thoracic, cardiovascular, abdominal, oncological, musculoskeletal, and interventional imaging, with particular attention to stroke, cancer, and other image-guided treatment. Other factors such as regulatory status, deployment status, and estimated technology readiness (TRL) level are compared. Current evidence indicates that mobile CT, telehealth, and AI for conventional CT are individually relatively mature, but fully integration of these technologies remains less widely deployed and validated in the clinical settings. Key barriers include regulatory variation, domain shift, connectivity requirements, cost, workflow integration, cybersecurity, and limited evidence of patient-level benefit. Future research should prioritise prospective, multicentre evaluation of integrated CT systems in real-world and underserved clinical settings.

</details>


### [93] [Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA for Multi-Task Medical Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14350)
*Zhanghao Chen,Yuanyuan Li,Zhenyu Lu,Shuo Gao,Guangquan Zhou,Yikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段LoRA混合框架（Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA），基于MedGemma-1.5-4B实现医学视觉语言模型的联合适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM需单模型完成诊断分类到报告生成等多任务，但联合适配面临输出格式异构、任务梯度冲突与训练数据不均衡三大挑战。

Method: 采用共享-专用混合LoRA架构：1个共享LoRA加6个任务专用专家LoRA，配合两阶段训练。第一阶段联合训练共享LoRA与所有专家LoRA；第二阶段冻结骨干、共享LoRA及非目标任务专家，逐个精调任务专家；分类与回归任务额外进行模态平衡延续训练，重复较小模态组以匹配最大组规模。

Result: 在FLARE 2026 Task 3测试集上，分类平衡准确率0.85，多标签分类micro-F1为0.48，检测F1为0.79，回归MAE为17.39。

Conclusion: 该框架通过共享-专用参数解耦与分阶段精调，有效缓解了多任务医学VLM适配中的梯度冲突与数据不均衡问题，在多类临床任务上取得均衡性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) allow a single model to perform clinical image analysis tasks ranging from diagnosis classification to report generation. However, joint adaptation is challenged by heterogeneous output formats, conflicting task gradients, and imbalanced training data. Hence, we present \textbf{Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA}, a framework built on MedGemma-1.5-4B. The framework uses a shared-specific Mixture-of-LoRA architecture comprising one shared LoRA and six task-specific expert LoRAs, together with a two-stage training procedure. In Stage 1, we jointly train the shared LoRA and all task-specific expert LoRAs on all tasks. In Stage 2, we first freeze the backbone, the shared LoRA, and all non-target experts, and refine one task expert at a time. Classification and regression then receive an additional modality-balanced continuation, in which smaller modality groups are repeated to match the largest group. In the FLARE 2026 Task 3 test sets, the proposed method achieves 0.85 balanced accuracy for classification, 0.48 micro-F1 for multi-label classification, 0.79 detection F1, and 17.39 regression MAE. Code is available at https://github.com/YuanYL03/MICCAI-FLARE-2026-Challenge-Task3-2D.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Natural Images: Rethinking AI-Generated Image Detection in Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14352)
*Zhangjie Fu,Jiazhen Yan,Yuanwen Chen,Xinquan Yu,Yanzhe Li,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文关注AI生成文档图像检测这一被忽视领域，构建诊断基准AIGDoc-Pilot揭示现有检测器性能显著下降（AUC平均降低超7%），并发现生成伪影空间不一致性、文本密度影响可分性两大特性，进而构建AIGDoc数据集，为文档场景可靠检测提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测评估主要聚焦自然图像，忽视文档图像。而文档常出现于发票、报销单、证书、医疗记录等敏感场景，检测缺失存在风险。因此需要针对AI生成文档图像开展系统评测与分析。

Method: 首先构建受控诊断基准AIGDoc-Pilot，评测现有检测器在AI生成文档图像上的表现，并分析两种文档特异性：生成伪影在局部区域存在强空间不一致性；文本密度显著影响真实-合成可分性，文本密集区域判别证据更强。基于此构建更大规模、以文档为核心的AIGDoc数据集，涵盖多样真实文档及由多种先进生成与编辑模型产出的AI生成对应物。

Result: AIGDoc-Pilot上现有检测器性能大幅下降，平均AUC降低超过7%。在AIGDoc上，现有检测器仍难以可靠识别AI生成文档，基于文档的训练可部分缩小差距。

Conclusion: 研究为文档场景下开发可靠、可泛化的AI生成图像检测器提供了有价值的洞见，代码与数据集将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection has attracted increasing attention, but existing evaluations mainly focus on natural images, leaving AI-generated document images largely underexplored. This omission is concerning because documents often appear in sensitive real-world scenarios, such as invoices, expense reports, certificates, and medical records. In this paper, we first construct a controlled diagnostic benchmark, AIGDoc-Pilot, and reveal that existing detectors suffer substantial performance degradation on AI-generated document images, with the mean AUC dropping by more than 7%. Based on this, we further reveal two document-specific properties behind this gap: generation artifacts exhibit strong spatial inconsistency across local regions, and text density significantly affects real-synthetic separability, where text-dense regions offer stronger discriminative evidence. Motivated by these findings, we construct AIGDoc, a larger document-centric dataset containing diverse real-world documents and AI-generated counterparts produced by multiple advanced generation and editing models. Extensive experiments on AIGDoc demonstrate that existing detectors still struggle to reliably identify AI-generated documents, while document-based training partially narrows the gap. Together, these results offer valuable insights for developing dependable and generalizable detectors in document-centric scenarios. The code and datasets will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [95] [Rethinking Camouflage Image Generation towards a Training-Free Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14377)
*Haodong Yang,Zhongling Huang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练伪装图像生成范式，并实现 FreeCam 框架，在无需参数更新下实现优于现有方法的质量与伪装效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CIG 方法多依赖伪装数据集上的任务特定训练，计算成本高且跨域泛化能力差；同时需同时满足前景保持、语义兼容与外观同化三重要求。

Method: 将 CIG 重新表述为免训练的"隐蔽导向"范式：保持目标同时降低其与合成环境的可分辨性。基于冻结的 inpainting 扩散框架实现 FreeCam：上下文推理模块利用冻结多模态先验推断利于隐蔽的环境以提升语义兼容性；内在外观模块从前景提取低级颜色与纹理线索以引导背景合成实现外观同化。

Result: 大量实验表明，FreeCam 在无任务特定训练下达到最先进的生成质量与伪装效果；生成图像可为伪装目标检测提供合成监督，并降低通用目标检测器下的目标可检测性。

Conclusion: 免训练、隐蔽导向的 CIG 范式可行且有效，FreeCam 在质量、伪装效果与下游应用上均具优势，克服了任务特定训练的成本与泛化限制。

Abstract: Camouflage image generation (CIG) aims to synthesize realistic camouflaged images by blending foreground objects into concealment-compatible background contexts. Achieving this objective requires jointly satisfying three coupled requirements: foreground preservation to retain target integrity, semantic compatibility to select plausible concealment contexts, and appearance assimilation to reduce visual discrepancies. Recent approaches predominantly rely on task-specific training on camouflage datasets to address these requirements, incurring substantial computational cost and limiting generalization beyond the training domain. To address these limitations, we formulate training-free CIG as a concealment-oriented paradigm that preserves the target while reducing its perceptual separability from the synthesized surroundings, rather than maintaining its visual prominence, without parameter updates. We instantiate this paradigm with FreeCam based on a frozen inpainting diffusion framework to preserve the foreground. Within this framework, a Contextual Reasoning Module exploits frozen multimodal priors to infer an environment favorable to concealment, thereby promoting semantic compatibility, while an Intrinsic Appearance Module extracts low-level color and texture cues from the foreground to guide background synthesis toward appearance assimilation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeCam achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and camouflage effectiveness without task-specific training, while its generated images provide synthetic supervision for camouflaged object detection and reduce target detectability under general object detectors.

</details>


### [96] [Contour-Guided Spectral Routing for Robust Real-Time Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14383)
*Sam Williams,Yuan Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轮廓引导的频谱路由框架，用于驾驶场景实时行人检测，通过统一的信号路由顺序处理小目标、遮挡和天气/光照变化三类失效模式，并结合小波子带训练变换，在CityPersons上取得优于RT-DETR的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景实时行人检测受三种耦合失效模式制约：小目标缺乏判别证据、遮挡削弱几何支撑、天气或光照变化扭曲外观统计。现有方法通常把频率处理、注意力和边界推理当作独立附加模块，缺乏统一视角，因此作者希望以统一的轮廓引导频谱路由框架来整体建模这些耦合问题。

Method: 将检测器建模为统一的轮廓引导频谱路由视角，按指定顺序路由信息：先为空间证据增强全局频谱上下文，再让深度表示交换空间与频谱线索，最后以边界—语义不一致性条件化跨尺度融合。该顺序形成紧凑表示流水线，低频上下文稳定全局结构，高频证据保护小目标轮廓。同时保留小波子带训练变换，独立扰动低频和高频系数以应对雾、雨、雪和低光照导致的外观变化。该表述在每个阶段暴露单一路由变量，并区分可复用信号变换与决定信号注入位置的任务特定策略。

Result: 在CityPersons上，所提检测器取得70.4 AP50和44.2 AP50:95，相比RT-DETR的68.1和42.2有所提升；完整小波增强配置达到71.1 AP50和44.6 AP50:95。

Conclusion: 以统一轮廓引导频谱路由顺序组织空间、频谱与边界信息，并配合小波子带训练变换，可有效缓解驾驶场景行人检测中的小目标、遮挡和天气/光照三类耦合失效模式，在CityPersons上优于RT-DETR基准，且小波增强配置进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Real-time pedestrian detection in driving scenes is constrained by three coupled failure modes: tiny targets lose discriminative evidence, occlusion weakens geometric support, and weather or illumination changes distort appearance statistics. We formulate the detector through a unified \emph{contour-guided spectral routing} view rather than treating frequency processing, attention, and boundary reasoning as independent add-ons. The detector routes information in a prescribed order: spatial evidence is first augmented with global spectral context, deep representations then exchange spatial and spectral cues, and cross-scale fusion is finally conditioned on boundary--semantic disagreement. This ordering yields a compact representation pipeline in which low-frequency context stabilizes global structure while high-frequency evidence protects small-object contours. We further retain a wavelet-subband training transformation that perturbs low- and high-frequency coefficients independently, targeting appearance shifts caused by fog, rain, snow, and low illumination. The formulation exposes a single routing variable at each stage and distinguishes reusable signal transforms from the task-specific policy that decides where each signal is injected. On CityPersons, the proposed detector obtains 70.4 AP$_{50}$ and 44.2 AP$_{50:95}$, compared with 68.1 and 42.2 for RT-DETR, while the full wavelet-augmented configuration reaches 71.1 and 44.6.

</details>


### [97] [Newton Deep Unfolding for Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14391)
*Changhua He,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于二阶优化的深度展开网络NDU-Net用于压缩感知重建，通过牛顿更新模块和多尺度先验模块提升性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度展开方法通常由一阶优化驱动，且对重建过程中产生的优化状态利用不足，限制了压缩感知重建的性能。

Method: 提出牛顿深度展开网络NDU-Net：引入牛顿更新（NU）模块估计牛顿型更新方向并生成刻画当前重建过程的优化状态；设计牛顿引导的多尺度先验（MP）模块，将这些优化状态融入多尺度特征恢复，使学习到的先验适应当前重建阶段。

Result: 在不同压缩感知比率下的实验表明，NDU-Net取得了有前景的重建性能并展现出更强的鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: NDU-Net是首个利用二阶优化进行压缩感知重建的深度展开框架，验证了二阶优化与优化状态引导先验在CS重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Compressed sensing (CS) reconstructs images from highly limited measurements, but existing deep unfolding methods are typically driven by first-order optimization and weakly exploit the optimization states generated during reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose a Newton deep unfolding network (NDU-Net), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first deep unfolding framework that leverages second-order optimization for CS reconstruction. Specifically, NDU-Net introduces a Newton update (NU) module to estimate Newton-type update directions and generate optimization states that characterize the current reconstruction process. Furthermore, a Newton-guided multi-scale prior (MP) module is designed to incorporate these optimization states into multi-scale feature restoration, thereby enabling the learned prior to adapt to the current reconstruction stage. Experimental results under different CS ratios confirm that our proposed NDU-Net achieves promising reconstruction performance and exhibits enhanced robustness. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DNU-Net.

</details>


### [98] [A Generative AI Integrated Multimodal Framework for Low-Latency Multi-Camera Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14419)
*Leon Fernando,C Dombawala,P. Hettigoda,Vanodhya G. Warnasooriya,Ishara Neranjana,Rashmika Nawaratne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式AI集成的多模态行人重识别框架，采用成本感知早退级联策略，在保持检索性能的同时降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人重识别对多摄像头监控与跟踪至关重要，但受视角与光照变化、遮挡、背景杂乱及低分辨率影响，仍具挑战性；同时需在缺失线索下保持鲁棒性并实现低延迟部署。

Method: 融合三类模态：(i) 分割行人区域的全局视觉嵌入；(ii) 视觉语言模型自动生成的细粒度语义属性描述；(iii) 可靠人脸观测时的可选人脸嵌入。采用成本感知早退级联替代全模态融合：先检查top-k检索结果判断查询是否无歧义，若最佳匹配明显分离则提前终止以降低延迟；对歧义情况保留多个假设，调用额外模态并以自适应可靠性加权进行细化。

Result: 在Market-1501和DukeMTMC-reID基准上以mAP和Rank-1评估。自适应早退级联在不调用语义推理的情况下解决了DukeMTMC-reID 60.7%和Market-1501 68.4%的查询，降低计算开销并保持有竞争力的检索性能。

Conclusion: 成本感知早退级联可在多数查询上避免昂贵的语义推理，实现准确率与延迟的平衡，是面向低延迟多模态行人重识别的有效方案。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) is essential for multi-camera surveillance and tracking, yet remains difficult due to viewpoint and illumination changes, occlusion, background clutter, and low resolution imagery. We propose a generative AI integrated multimodal ReID framework designed explicitly for robustness under missing cues and low latency deployment. The key idea is a cost aware early-exit cascade that prioritizes inexpensive, high confidence evidence and only triggers expensive modalities for ambiguous cases.
  Our system integrates (i) global visual embeddings from segmented person regions, (ii) automatically generated fine grained semantic attribute descriptions generated by vision-language models (VLMs), and (iii) optional facial embeddings when face observations are reliable. To optimize the balance between accuracy and latency, we use a cost aware early-exit cascade instead of fusing all modalities. Specifically, we first inspect the top-k retrieval results to determine whether the query is unambiguous. If the best match is clearly separated from the remaining candidates, we stop early and return the result to minimize latency; in ambiguous cases, we keep multiple hypotheses and invoke additional modalities (face/semantic) with adaptive reliability weighting to refine the decision.
  We report person re-identification performance using mAP and Rank-1 accuracy on the Market-1501 and DukeMTMC-reID benchmarks. The proposed adaptive early-exit cascade resolves 60.7% of DukeMTMC-reID queries and 68.4% of Market-1501 queries without invoking semantic reasoning, reducing computational overhead while maintaining competitive retrieval performance.

</details>


### [99] [Lightweight Generalized DeepFake Face Detection with WAVIE: Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14437)
*Arya Pulkit,Aditya Ruhela,Akarshan Kapoor,Arnav Bhavsar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAVIE 在冻结的 CLIP 骨干上融合空间与频率线索，通过小波变换增强中间特征，仅在 FaceForensics++ 训练即可在多个未见数据集上取得较强的深度伪造检测泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测器在训练所用的伪造方法上表现良好，但在未见过的伪造流程上准确率急剧下降，泛化能力不足，限制了其在虚假信息治理、数字取证和人本 AI 系统中的实际部署可靠性。

Method: 提出 WAVIE（Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings）端到端架构：基于冻结的 CLIP 骨干，将中间 Transformer 嵌入通过轻量可学习模块投影，施加三级 Daubechies-6（db6）离散小波变换，对低频分支进行细化并保留高频分支，再经逆小波变换重建特征并分类。

Result: 仅在 FaceForensics++ 上训练，帧级 AUROC 达到 Celeb-DF-v1 0.852、Celeb-DF-v2 0.852、WildDeepFake 0.831，优于多个现有泛化基线；消融实验证实小波模块与中间特征聚合对跨数据集性能均具重要作用。

Conclusion: 联合利用空间与频率域信息对跨数据集深度伪造检测至关重要，WAVIE 可作为野外深度伪造检测的强基线。

Abstract: Deepfake detection systems often exhibit significant performance degradation when deployed on unseen manipulation methods, limiting their reliability in real-world multimedia environments. This lack of generalization poses critical challenges for misinformation mitigation, digital forensics, and human-centric AI systems. Existing detectors perform well on the forgery methods they are trained on, but their accuracy drops sharply on unseen pipelines. To bridge this generalization gap, we propose WAVIE (Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings), an end-to-end architecture that combines complementary spatial and frequency cues on top of a frozen CLIP backbone. WAVIE projects intermediate transformer embeddings through a lightweight learnable module, applies a three-level Daubechies-6 (db6) discrete wavelet transform (DWT), refines the low-frequency branch while preserving the high-frequency branch, reconstructs the feature via inverse DWT, and performs classification.
  Trained only on FaceForensics++, WAVIE achieves AUROC = 0.852 on Celeb-DF-v1, 0.852 on Celeb-DF-v2 and 0.831 on WildDeepFake (WDF) at the frame level, outperforming several state-of-the-art generalization baselines. Extensive ablation studies confirm the importance of both the wavelet module and the intermediate-feature aggregation for cross-dataset performance, highlighting the necessity of jointly leveraging spatial and frequency domains. These results position WAVIE as a strong baseline for deepfake detection in the wild.

</details>


### [100] [Context-Aware Mutual Learning for Blind Image Inpainting and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14439)
*Haoru Zhao,Yufeng Wang,Zhaorui Gu,Bing Zheng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知互学习（CAML）框架，联合掩码估计与图像修复，通过双向互学习挖掘上下文信息，在盲图像修复及去雪、去影、去水印任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲图像修复在掩码未知时极具挑战。现有两阶段方法虽通过显式估计掩码提升了修复质量与泛化能力，但过度强调掩码估计对图像修复的单向作用，忽略了二者之间的相互促进关系：掩码估计可为修复提供更多上下文语义，修复可为掩码估计提供更多上下文细节。

Method: 提出上下文感知互学习（CAML）框架，将掩码估计与图像修复联合，实现上下文信息的相互利用。其中设计修复引导的上下文互学习器（IGCM），从图像修复中获取互补的上下文细节以辅助掩码估计；设计估计引导的上下文互学习器（EGCM），从掩码估计中强化上下文语义理解以辅助图像修复。

Result: 消融实验验证了CAML的有效性；大量实验表明CAML在盲图像修复以及去雪、去影、去水印等额外视觉任务上均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过双向互学习建立掩码估计与图像修复的相互促进关系，CAML有效利用了两阶段的上下文信息，展现出优越性并具有良好的推广能力。

Abstract: Blind image inpainting, aiming to recover contaminated images in the case of unknown masks, is a challenging task. Motivated by the perspective of human vision and knowledge, blind image inpainting can be decomposed into two stages: mask estimation and image inpainting based on the estimated mask. The two-stage idea exhibits evident advantages in enhancing inpainting quality and augmenting the generalization capability of unknown real-world contamination by explicitly employing the estimated mask for image inpainting compared to one-stage scheme. This two-stage idea has also been intuitively implemented. However, existing two-stage methods excessively emphasize the unilateral relationship of mask estimation to image inpainting, and may overlook the mutual relations between them. Specifically, mask estimation can provide more contextual semantics for image inpainting to strengthen the understanding of semantics, and image inpainting can offer more contextual details (e.g., textures and edges) for mask estimation to improve the learning of details. In this work, we propose a novel Context-Aware Mutual Learning (CAML) framework for blind image inpainting that joints mask estimation and image inpainting to mutually exploit contextual information. In the CAML framework, we design the Inpainting-Guided Context-Mutual (IGCM) learner to acquire the complementary contextual details from image inpainting for assisting mask estimation, and the Estimation-Guided Context-Mutual (EGCM) learner to strengthen the understanding of contextual semantics from mask estimation for assisting image inpainting. Ablation studies validate the efficacy of our CAML. Extensive experiments show that our CAML achieves state-of-the-art performance on both blind image inpainting and additional vision tasks, i.e., snow removal, shadow removal, and watermark removal, indicating its superiority.

</details>


### [101] [From Visual Attribution to Clinical Reasoning: Explainable Parkinson's Disease Screening from Hand-Drawn Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14441)
*Aritra Dey,Utsav Kumar Nareti,Chandranath Adak,Soumi Chattopadhyay,Krishna Gopal Sasmal,Saeed Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释框架，通过离线手绘图案（螺旋、回纹）筛查帕金森病，结合判别式视觉建模与临床推理，并用多阶段可解释性流程给出临床一致的诊断依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病早期神经运动损伤可在手绘图案中表现为震颤振荡、笔画不规则和曲率不稳定。现有基于手绘图案的筛查多为黑箱模型，缺乏临床可解释性，难以支撑临床信任与应用。

Method: 采用判别式视觉模型捕捉分布式结构畸变与细粒度纹理变化，并在受试者不交叉（subject-disjoint）协议下评估。构建多阶段可解释流程：先用注意力与梯度定位显著区域，再提取轮廓粗糙度、曲率不规则性、笔画变异性和震颤频率能量等临床运动描述符，最后通过语言推理模块将其转化为连贯的临床解释。

Result: 在公开帕金森病手写数据集上，该框架取得具有竞争力的预测性能，并生成与临床一致的解释。

Conclusion: 该框架将视觉归因与临床解释相衔接，推动了基于手绘图案的神经系统筛查中的可解释文档智能发展。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) manifests early neuromotor impairments that become observable in controlled hand-drawn patterns such as spirals and meanders, where tremor-induced oscillations, stroke irregularity, and curvature instability reflect underlying motor degradation. In this work, we present an explainable framework for PD screening from offline hand-drawn patterns that integrates discriminative visual modeling with clinically grounded reasoning. The predictive model captures distributed structural distortions and fine-grained texture variations. It is evaluated under subject-disjoint protocols to ensure reliable generalization. To move beyond black-box classification, we introduce a multi-stage explainability pipeline that combines visual attribution with structured symptom abstraction. Salient regions are identified using attention- and gradient-based localization, followed by extraction of clinically meaningful motor descriptors quantifying contour roughness, curvature irregularity, stroke variability, and tremor-frequency energy. These descriptors are subsequently translated into coherent clinical rationales through a language-based reasoning module, linking model evidence to established PD symptomatology. By bridging visual attribution and clinical interpretation, the proposed framework advances interpretable document intelligence for neurological screening using hand-drawn patterns. Experimental results on publicly available Parkinson's disease handwriting datasets demonstrate competitive predictive performance and clinically consistent explanations.

</details>


### [102] [DynEoMT: Learning Object Dynamicity from Online Segmentation Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14466)
*Calvin Galagain,Martyna Poreba,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynEoMT 是一个在线视频分割框架，能同时输出分割结果并预测每个区域的动态/静态状态，仅用当前帧和传播的查询即可实现，无需光流、深度、相机位姿等信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分割模型能识别和跟踪目标，但不判断分割区域是否独立于相机运动而运动；该动态性属性无法仅从语义推断，且受相机自运动干扰，缺乏相关标注基准。

Method: 在基于查询的视频分割中加入区域级动态性预测，联合输出分割结果与每个区域的动态/静态状态；推理时只用当前帧和传播查询，不用光流、深度、相机位姿、历史帧或特征图；并提出类别无关的离线监督流程，用相机补偿光流和置信度感知时间滤波生成监督。

Result: 在 VIPSeg、OVIS、YouTube-VIS 2022、VSPW 上分别取得 84.3、68.0、68.6、87.6 的平衡准确率，同时基本保持原有分割性能。

Conclusion: 分割区域动态性可从传播查询中学习，无需推理阶段专门的运动处理流程即可在线预测；完整代码将开源以支持复现。

Abstract: Video segmentation models recognize and track objects over time, but they do not indicate whether each segmented region moves independently of the observing camera. This dynamicity attribute cannot be inferred from semantics alone and is confounded by camera ego-motion. We introduce \method, an online framework that augments query-based video segmentation with region-level dynamicity prediction. It jointly produces the original segmentation outputs and a dynamic or static state for each predicted region. At inference, DynEoMT uses only the current frame and propagated queries, without optical flow, depth, camera pose, previous RGB frames, or feature maps. Because established video segmentation benchmarks do not annotate this attribute, we also introduce a class-agnostic offline supervision pipeline using camera-compensated optical flow and confidence-aware temporal filtering. Across VIPSeg, OVIS, YouTube-VIS 2022, and VSPW, DynEoMT achieves balanced accuracies of 84.3, 68.0, 68.6, and 87.6, respectively, while largely preserving segmentation performance. These results show that segmentation-region dynamicity can be learned from propagated queries, enabling its online prediction without a dedicated motion-processing pipeline at inference. The complete code will be released as open source to enable full reproduction of the method and experiments.

</details>


### [103] [PRI-Net: A Lightweight Multimodal Framework for 3D UAV Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14469)
*Zhixuan Chen,Jialiang Lu,Zhong Ye,Yinghui He,Guanding Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRI-Net，一个轻量高效的多模态融合框架，通过点云泼溅、残差注意力融合和信息瓶颈实现无人机高精度3D定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机多模态3D定位方法面临三大挑战：LiDAR几何稀疏、模态融合不均衡、以及边缘到服务器链路带宽受限下的冗余特征传输。目标是同时提升定位精度与传输效率。

Method: PRI-Net集成三个模块：(1) 3D点云泼溅(3DPCS)策略，将稀疏LiDAR观测转化为几何一致的稠密深度图；(2) 残差注意力融合(RAF)模块，用图像分支做粗估计、门控融合分支做细化，缓解模态偏差；(3) 多模态信息瓶颈(MIB)模块，过滤任务无关冗余以压缩特征。

Result: 实验表明，PRI-Net以轻量架构实现高定位精度，同时降低特征维度，提升了边缘到服务器的无人机感知效率与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PRI-Net通过点云稠密化、注意力融合与信息瓶颈的协同设计，在精度与效率之间取得平衡，适用于带宽受限的边缘-服务器无人机感知场景。

Abstract: Accurate 3D localization of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging for existing multimodal approaches due to sparse LiDAR geometry, modality-imbalanced fusion, and redundant feature transmission over constrained edge-to-server links. To address these limitations, we propose PRI-Net, an efficient and lightweight multimodal fusion framework for UAV localization that integrates point cloud splatting, residual attention fusion, and an information bottleneck. Specifically, a 3D point cloud splatting (3DPCS) strategy is introduced to transform sparse LiDAR observations into geometrically consistent dense depth maps. A residual attention fusion (RAF) module is then designed to alleviate modal bias by using an image branch for coarse estimation and a gated fusion branch for refinement. In addition, a multimodal information bottleneck (MIB) module compacts features by filtering task-irrelevant redundancy. Experiments show that PRI-Net achieves high localization accuracy with lightweight architectures, while reducing feature dimensionality and improving edge-to-server UAV sensing efficiency and robustness.

</details>


### [104] [PuzzleMate: Benchmarking MLLMs for Egocentric Puzzle Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14473)
*Avijit Dasgupta,Shayon Dasgupta,Zakaria Laskar,C. V. Jawahar,Karteek Alahari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以拼图任务为测试平台，提出PuzzleMate框架，评估多模态大模型在自我中心视角下的细粒度顺序指导能力，发现七大瓶颈并构建基准，揭示当前模型在拼图推理上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI助手正从数字界面演化为具身伙伴，需为用户提供与实时进度匹配的精确分步指导。尽管多模态大模型在通用视觉理解上表现良好，但其对细粒度操作任务提供有依据的顺序指导能力尚未得到验证，因此需要合适任务进行系统考察。

Method: 选择拼图拼装作为测试平台，因其要求高精度空间推理、辨别细微几何差异及严格顺序逻辑。提出PuzzleMate框架，以自我中心视角采集拼图过程，并在用户参与循环（user-in-the-loop）研究中部署，评估最先进MLLM对当前拼图状态的感知与可执行下一步指令生成能力。基于发现的七大瓶颈，进一步构建系统性评估基准。

Result: 分析揭示限制MLLM有效性的七大瓶颈；在构建的基准上评估发现，GPT-5.2与Gemini-2.5-Pro等当前模型存在显著性能差距，难以处理拼图辅助所需的复杂推理与顺序逻辑。

Conclusion: 尽管MLLM能力强大，但在细粒度空间推理与顺序逻辑指导方面仍有明显不足，拼图任务可作为检验和改进该类能力的有效基准，为具身AI助手的实用化指明改进方向。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants hold the potential to evolve from digital interfaces into embodied companions capable of guiding users through complex physical activities. For these assistants to become integral to daily life, they must do more than identify objects; they must provide precise, step-by-step instructions that align with a user's real-time progress. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show promise in general visual understanding, their ability to deliver grounded, sequential guidance for fine-grained manipulation tasks remains largely unverified.
  In this paper, we choose the jigsaw puzzle as a strategic testbed for this capability. Unlike general object recognition, puzzle solving demands high-precision spatial reasoning, the ability to distinguish between minute geometric variations, and a rigorous adherence to sequential logic.
  We investigate this capability through PuzzleMate, a novel framework focused on jigsaw puzzle solving captured through an egocentric viewpoint. We deploy PuzzleMate in a user-in-the-loop study to evaluate how well state-of-the-art MLLMs perceive the current puzzle state and generate actionable next-step instructions. Our analysis reveals seven key bottlenecks that limit their effectiveness. Building on these insights, we propose a benchmark that enables systematic evaluation of MLLMs' reasoning capabilities for puzzle solving. Our findings reveal a substantial performance gap in current models like GPT-5.2 and Gemini-2.5-Pro; while these MLLMs are highly capable, they struggle to navigate the intricate reasoning and sequential logic essential for jigsaw puzzle assistance.

</details>


### [105] [MCIQA-2K: A Multi-Dimensional Dataset and No-Reference Quality Assessment Benchmark for Colorized Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14495)
*Yunkai Zhuang,Qihang Yan,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MCIQA-2K大规模多维度基准数据集，用于黑白图像着色结果的无参考质量评估，并设计了多分支NR-IQA框架MCIQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像着色是病态问题，同一灰度图可对应多种合理着色结果，传统全参考IQA指标无法准确反映人类对彩色化图像的感知偏好，因此需要专门的无参考质量评估基准与方法。

Method: 构建包含2000张由五种代表性着色模型生成的彩色化图像数据集，并从色彩涂抹、语义颜色错位、全局自然度三个感知维度进行人工标注；在此基础上提出多分支无参考IQA框架MCIQA。

Result: 大量实验表明，MCIQA在所提基准上显著优于现有的全参考与无参考IQA方法，同时在多个常用IQA数据集上展现出有竞争力的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MCIQA-2K基准与MCIQA框架为着色图像的无参考质量评估提供了有效方案，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Image colorization is an inherently ill-posed task, since a single grayscale image may correspond to multiple plausible colorized results. Consequently, conventional full-reference image quality assessment (IQA) metrics fail to accurately reflect human perceptual preferences for colorized images. In this paper, we present MCIQA-2K, a large-scale multi-dimensional benchmark specifically designed for no-reference quality assessment of colorized images. We construct a dataset containing 2,000 colorized images generated by five representative colorization models, together with human annotations across three perceptual dimensions: color smearing, semantic color misalignment, and global naturalness. Building upon the proposed benchmark, we further introduce MCIQA, a dedicated multi-branch NR-IQA framework for colorized images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MCIQA significantly outperforms existing full-reference and no-reference IQA methods on the proposed benchmark, while also exhibiting competitive generalization capability on several widely-used IQA datasets. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/ARBEZ-ZEBRA/MCIQA.

</details>


### [106] [CGGT: Curve-Grounded Geometry Transformer for 3D Parametric Curve Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14521)
*Zhirui Gao,Renjiao Yi,Yunfan Ye,Ruizhen Hu,Chenyang Zhu,Wei Chen,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CGGT，一个曲线接地的几何Transformer，能从稀疏、无位姿多视图图像中直接恢复可编辑3D参数曲线，并发布Wireframe-100K大规模数据集，在精度与效率上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从2D图像恢复可编辑3D参数曲线是计算机图形学中连接像素感知与矢量CAD建模的基础难题。现有基于NeRF和3DGS的方法依赖密集标定视图、预计算2D边缘图和昂贵的逐场景优化，难以适用于随意拍摄的真实输入。

Method: CGGT结合几何感知的Transformer编码器进行多视图特征学习，以及曲线感知掩码注意力解码器进行跨视图实例关联。单次前向传播即可预测相机参数、稠密深度图和实例级2D曲线掩码，随后提升到3D并经快速参数化优化细化，恢复紧凑可编辑的3D曲线基元。同时构建Wireframe-100K数据集，含10万个CAD模型、多视图渲染与参数曲线标注。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在重建精度和效率上均有显著提升，尤其在稀疏视图等挑战性场景下，并能有效区分持久性3D结构边缘与由轮廓、纹理和外观变化引起的视角相关图像边缘。仅在合成数据上训练即可良好泛化到真实图像。

Conclusion: CGGT为实现从无约束视觉输入进行实用CAD风格线框重建提供了潜力。

Abstract: Recovering editable 3D parametric curves from 2D images is a fundamental challenge in computer graphics, bridging pixel-based perception and vector-based CAD modeling. Existing NeRF- and 3DGS-based methods often rely on dense calibrated views, precomputed 2D edge maps, and costly per-scene optimization, limiting their applicability to casually captured real-world inputs. We propose CGGT, a Curve-Grounded Geometry Transformer that directly grounds 3D-consistent 2D curve instances in the image space from sparse, unposed multi-view images. CGGT combines a geometry-aware transformer encoder for multi-view feature learning with a curve-aware masked-attention decoder for cross-view instance association. In a single forward pass, it predicts camera parameters, dense depth maps, and instance-level 2D curve masks, which are then lifted into 3D and refined through a fast parametric optimization stage to recover compact, editable 3D curve primitives. To support structured curve learning, we introduce Wireframe-100K, a large-scale dataset comprising 100,000 CAD models with diverse topologies, realistic multi-view renderings, and accurate parametric curve annotations. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves substantial improvements in both reconstruction accuracy and efficiency, particularly under challenging sparse-view settings and in separating persistent 3D structural edges from view-dependent image edges caused by silhouettes, textures, and appearance variations. Despite being trained solely on synthetic data, CGGT generalizes well to real-world images, demonstrating its potential for practical CAD-style wireframe reconstruction from unconstrained visual inputs.

</details>


### [107] [Selective Tool Use for Agentic Change Visual Question Answering in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14523)
*Yakoub Bazi,Mohamad M. Al Rahhal,Mohamed A. Mekhtiche,Mansour Zuair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性工具调用框架，让单一VLM在直接作答或调用确定性变化分析工具之间选择，配合工具增强的CDVQA数据，将Change VQA准确率从73.77%提升至88.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Change VQA需要理解双时相遥感影像的语义变化，但现有视觉语言模型在涉及显式转换统计、面积测量或空间信息的问题上表现不可靠。

Method: 构建选择性工具使用框架：单个VLM决定直接回答或调用确定性变化分析工具；工具在双时相语义图上运行并返回结构化观测，再由同一VLM生成最终答案。构建CDVQA的工具增强扩展，覆盖8类问题及转换、空间、时间三类工具，工具监督与观测由原始语义标注自动导出，无需额外人工标注；用LoRA微调Qwen3.5-4B联合学习直接作答、工具调用与证据条件作答。

Result: 在7,164个测试问题上，使用参考语义图时总体准确率从73.77%提升至88.79%，家族平均准确率从69.11%提升至89.65%；语义图自动预测时总体准确率为77.47%，家族平均准确率为75.06%。

Conclusion: 问题特定的语义证据对Change VQA有显著收益，同时语义预测质量会明显影响最终性能。代码与工具增强标注将公开。

Abstract: Change visual question answering (Change VQA) requires understanding semantic changes across bi-temporal remote sensing images. Although vision language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on this task, they remain unreliable when answering questions that require explicit transition statistics, area measurements, or spatial information. To address this limitation, we propose a selective tool use framework in which a single VLM either answers directly or invokes a deterministic change analysis tool to obtain question specific evidence. Specifically, the selected tool operates on bi-temporal semantic maps and returns a structured observation, which the same VLM uses to generate its final answer. To support this framework, we construct a tool augmented extension of CDVQA covering eight question families and three tools for transition, spatial, and temporal analysis. Tool use supervision and observations are derived automatically from the original semantic annotations, without additional manual labeling. We then adapt Qwen3.5-4B using Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to jointly learn direct answering, tool invocation, and evidence conditioned answering. Experiments on 7,164 test questions show that selective tool use with reference semantic maps improves overall accuracy from 73.77% to 88.79% and average family accuracy from 69.11% to 89.65%. When the semantic maps are predicted automatically, the framework achieves 77.47% overall accuracy and 75.06% average family accuracy. These results demonstrate the benefit of question-specific semantic evidence for Change VQA, while highlighting the influence of semantic prediction quality on the resulting performance. Code and tool-augmented annotations will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yakoubbazi/ToolChangeVQA.

</details>


### [108] [Sharing standardized image-derived data in computational pathology using DICOM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14530)
*Daniela P. Schacherer,Christopher P. Bridge,David Clunie,Igor Octaviano,André Homeyer,Markus D. Herrmann,Olivier Gevaert,Tabita Ghete,Markus Metzler,Henning Hoefener,Tahsin Kurc,Curtis Lisle,Kenneth Philbrick,Joel Saltz,Yuanning Zheng,Andrey Fedorov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出在NCI影像数据共享平台IDC中，以DICOM标准对病理图像衍生数据（如感兴趣区标注、分割掩膜）进行编码与共享，并展示五个代表性数据集的统一化实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学方法的开发与评估需要大规模、多样化的数据集。过去十年间，病理成像原始数据的收集与共享已有大量投入，但图像衍生数据（如感兴趣区勾画、分割掩膜）的共享发展不足。同时，IDC依赖DICOM标准进行数据统一化，而DICOM在病理图像衍生内容中的应用此前基本未被探索。

Method: 依托NCI影像数据共享平台IDC，采用DICOM标准对病理图像衍生数据进行编码与统一化。将五个代表性数据集从其原始表示形式转换/统一为DICOM格式，并在IDC中公开共享，同时为关键开源工具做出贡献。

Result: 完成五个代表性数据集的DICOM统一化并公开共享，展示了该统一化方式的优势，介绍了相关开源工具的开发贡献。

Conclusion: 在IDC中以DICOM标准化编码和共享病理图像衍生数据具有明显优势，并讨论了DICOM在病理图像衍生数据中更广泛采用所涉及的技术考量。

Abstract: Development and evaluation of computational pathology methods require access to large and diverse datasets. Over the past decade, various initiatives invested significantly into collecting, centralizing, and sharing pathology imaging data. In contrast, sharing of image-derived data such as region-of-interest delineations or segmentation masks is less well developed. In this work, we describe our approach to encoding and sharing image-derived pathology data in a standardized manner within the National Cancer Institute (NCI) Imaging Data Commons (IDC), a platform that hosts and provides public access to de-identified radiology and pathology data. The IDC relies on the Digital Imaging and Communications in Medicine (DICOM) standard for data harmonization, yet the adoption of DICOM for pathology image-derived content has remained largely unexplored until now. Here, we present five representative datasets harmonized by conversion from their original representations into DICOM and shared publicly in the IDC. We demonstrate the benefits of this harmonization, describe contributions to critical open-source tooling, and discuss technical considerations relevant to broader adoption of DICOM for pathology image-derived data.

</details>


### [109] [Small Object Detection in Drone Aerial Imagery with LAF-YOLOv10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14560)
*Quratulain Nayeem,Fahmina Taranum,Mohammed Mudassir Uddin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAF-YOLOv10将四种独立有效的架构技术组合进YOLOv10n用于无人机小目标检测，但组合后性能不升反降，低于基线7.8个mAP点，表明技术组合的可组合性不能想当然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用目标检测器在无人机影像上精度下降（目标仅几像素、机载算力有限）。已有工作将各自独立验证过的架构技术直接拼接成一个检测器，并假设单独报告的增益在组合后可以迁移，本文旨在直接检验这一假设。

Method: 将四项技术集成进YOLOv10n：PC-C2f骨干块、AG-FPN、用P2检测头替换大目标P5头、Wise-IoU v3回归。在VisDrone-DET2019上以三个随机种子训练，与原始YOLOv10n对比，并使用TIDE误差分解、逐类别分解、逐组件消融、注意力/损失对比、零样本迁移到UAVDT以及留出集/test-dev评估来定位成败来源。

Result: LAF-YOLOv10仅达24.0±0.4% mAP@0.5（2.14M参数），比YOLOv10n的31.8%低7.8点，该差距在UAVDT上转移为-10.0点，并被留出集与test-dev确认（23.5%、22.5%）。背景误检、定位误差和重复检测均向AG-FPN和Wise-IoU原本要改善的反方向移动。消融将缺陷归因于P2/-P5头替换（单独损失2.5点，叠加在被PC-C2f削弱的骨干上再产生2.5点交互惩罚），PC-C2f自身2.0点损失与部分预训练权重迁移（150个骨干张量中仅73个迁移）一致。

Conclusion: 该失败源于特定组件间的交互作用，而非四个组件各自本身；可组合性必须被直接验证，不能被默认假设。

Abstract: General-purpose object detectors lose accuracy on UAV footage, where targets span only a handful of pixels and onboard compute is limited. Prior work composes independently-validated architectural techniques into one detector, assuming gains reported in isolation transfer once combined. We stress-test that assumption directly. LAF-YOLOv10 integrates four techniques into YOLOv10n: a Partial Convolution C2f (PC-C2f) backbone block, an Attention-Guided Feature Pyramid Network (AG-FPN), a P2 detection head replacing the large-object P5 head, and Wise-IoU v3 regression, asking whether their combined effect matches what each contributes alone. We train LAF-YOLOv10 three times (seeds 42, 123, 256) on VisDrone-DET2019, benchmark against unmodified YOLOv10n, and use TIDE error decomposition, per-category breakdown, per-component ablation, attention/loss comparisons, zero-shot transfer to UAVDT, and held-out/test-dev evaluation to localize where the combination succeeds or fails. Composability does not hold here. LAF-YOLOv10 reaches 24.0+/-0.4% mAP@0.5 at 2.14M parameters, 7.8 points below YOLOv10n (31.8%), a deficit that transfers to UAVDT (-10.0 points) and is confirmed by held-out and test-dev evaluation (23.5%, 22.5%). Background false positives, localization error, and duplicate detections move in the direction AG-FPN and Wise-IoU were designed to push. Ablation traces the deficit to a specific source: the P2/-P5 head swap costs 2.5 points independently plus a 2.5-point interaction penalty when layered onto a backbone weakened by PC-C2f, whose own 2.0-point loss is consistent with a partial pretrained-weight transplant (73/150 backbone tensors transfer). The failure is attributable to a specific interaction, not the four components individually. Composability must be verified directly, not assumed. Code/checkpoints: https://github.com/Mudassiruddin7/Small-Object-Detection-in-UAV-Imagery.

</details>


### [110] [A Building as a Repository: KIR, a Typed Intermediate Representation for Agent-Authored Building Information Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14578)
*Dmitry Kuklev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KIR——一种带类型的中间表示，将建筑信息模型作为版本化仓库中的程序编写，并编译到宿主应用（如Revit）。KIR围绕生成器在"有状态、部分可观测宿主"中七类静默失败设计数据表示，实验表明其能以诊断码拒绝大量压力测试程序并静态识别不可见证义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体编写建筑信息模型时，仅访问宿主API远远不够。它们需要表征：自己的意图、编译器代表其做出的决定、被拒绝的请求、执行后观测到的结果，以及仍然未知的部分。现有方式缺乏对这类有状态、部分可观测宿主中失败模式的显式表示。

Method: KIR是一种带类型的中间表示：将建筑作为程序存放于版本化仓库，并作为构建目标下沉到宿主应用。它围绕生成器在宿主中悄然出错的七种方式，为每种情形设计数据表示：模糊选择器转为带候选的带类型拒绝；默认值保留其来源；不会被检查的义务在执行前被命名；空洞见证被静态拒绝；丢失的事务响应变为UNCONFIRMED状态并附"重试前验证"规则；反向路径遵循普查不变量；决策通过摘要与其所依据的状态绑定。

Result: 在固定快照（83条操作契约、仅Revit后端）上：离线实验在42个压力测试程序中拒绝29个（带诊断码、无未捕获异常）；将377条见证义务中的38条命名为不可见证；在七条不变量下，100种执行/见证/接受状态组合中有31种被接纳；219次证书运行未发现空洞见证。60层塔楼以KIR表示为11,263字符，而生成的C#为3,709,235字符。原生Revit运行结果来自项目记录，与复现结果分列。与直接编写宿主代码的智能体的受控对比已设计但尚未执行。

Conclusion: KIR通过为七类静默失败提供数据化表示，显著压缩了建筑模型表示体积并提升了失败诊断能力。与直接编写宿主代码的智能体的受控对比是当前的主要未决问题。

Abstract: Autonomous agents that author building information models need more than access to a host API. They need a representation of what they intended, what a compiler decided on their behalf, what was refused, what was observed after execution and what remains unknown. We present KIR, a typed intermediate representation in which a building is authored as a program held in a versioned repository and lowered to host applications as build targets. KIR is organised around seven ways in which a generator writing into a stateful, partially observable host goes silently wrong, and gives each a representation in data: ambiguous selectors become typed refusals with candidates; defaults keep their provenance; obligations that will not be checked are named before execution; vacuous witnesses are rejected statically; a lost transaction response becomes the state UNCONFIRMED with a verify-before-retry rule; the reverse path obeys a census invariant; and decisions are bound by digest to the state they were made against. On a pinned snapshot with 83 operation contracts and Revit as the only backend, offline experiments refuse 29 of 42 stress-test programs with diagnostic codes and no uncaught exception, name 38 of 377 witness obligations as unwitnessable, admit 31 of 100 combinations of execution, witness and acceptance states under seven stated invariants, and find no vacuous witness in 219 certificate runs; a 60-storey tower is 11,263 characters as KIR against 3,709,235 characters of emitted C#. Native Revit runs are reported from project records and kept separate from reproduced results. A controlled comparison with agents that write host code directly is specified but not yet executed; it is the principal open question.

</details>


### [111] [Reverse Spatio-Temporal Disease Progression Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14590)
*Ulugbek Shernazarov,Moucheng Xu,Inomjon Ramatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"反向疾病进展预测"任务：给定后期病变影像，重建此前未观测到的更健康解剖状态，用VQ自编码器+神经常微分方程在隐空间做连续时间反向积分，在Morpho-MNIST与ADNI上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习时空疾病进展模型几乎都是"前向"的，从观测基线预测未来衰退，忽视了疾病潜伏期，难以支持早期干预。而在临床中，影像往往在病理被怀疑或已可见后才开始采集，患者更健康的早期参照从未获得。因此需要研究反向预测，从后期病变状态回溯更健康的前期状态，以助力发现潜伏期并支持基于影像的早期干预。

Method: 采用两阶段模型：冻结的3D矢量量化（VQ）自编码器定义紧凑的离散隐空间；神经ODE在该隐空间中学习连续时间动力学。循环编码器按反向时间顺序读取后期观测，初始化隐状态，ODE沿轨迹反向积分以重建更早的健康状态。

Result: 在带正弦扰动的受控Morpho-MNIST基准上，模型成功从非单调轨迹的后期观测中恢复了未见的先前状态。在ADNI纵向脑MRI上，从已观测的后期病变状态恢复此前未见的、朝向健康状态的轨迹任务中，模型优于copy-nearest和mean-observed基线，并在每个诊断分层中均取得正的疾病逆转评分。

Conclusion: 该工作为从单次扫描中发现疾病潜伏期、以及基于影像开发疾病早期干预提供了思路与工具。

Abstract: Deep learning-based spatio-temporal disease progression models commonly overlook the incubation period of progressive diseases, limiting the use of those models in early interventions, which are vital for not easily reversible diseases such as Alzheimer's. This is because, the existing deep learning based longitudinal disease-progression models are almost always run forward: from an observed baseline they predict future decline. In many clinical settings, however, imaging begins only after pathology is suspected or already visible, the earlier, healthier patient-specific reference was never acquired. To address this, we propose to study reverse disease progression prediction: given later diseased anatomy, reconstruct the unobserved healthier anatomy that preceded it. We use a two-stage model in which a frozen 3D vector-quantised autoencoder defines a compact discrete latent space, while a Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) learns continuous-time dynamics in that space. A recurrent encoder reads late observations in reverse temporal order, initialises the latent state, and the ODE is integrated backwards across the trajectory. On a controlled Morpho-MNIST benchmark with a sinusoidal perturbation, our model successfully recovered the unseen previous states from later observations of the non-monotonic trajectory. On longitudinal brain MRIs from Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative, at the task to recover the previous unseen trajectory towards healthy states of the patients from observed later diseased states, our model outperforms the baselines that uses copy-nearest and mean-observed, with positive disease-reversal scores in every diagnostic stratum. We hope that our work can provide insights and tools towards discovering the incubation periods from single-shot scans, and developing early interventions of diseases based on imaging.

</details>


### [112] [Direct Conditional Transition Sampling for Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14596)
*Qi Yu,Hanlin Wu,Xiaohui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCTS（直接条件转移采样），一种无需训练的扩散逆问题求解器，通过随机流近似实现全局后验刷新，在四个逆问题上取得有竞争力的重建质量，最高实现16.8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练扩散逆求解器面临两难：局部测量引导方法缺乏全局校正能力，而干净空间的后验更新虽能改善全局校正，但需要概率流ODE积分与干净空间MCMC，计算代价高昂。因此需要一种能实现同样理想刷新目标、又避免昂贵计算的方法。

Method: DCTS直接对理想刷新目标进行随机流近似：不显式采样干净样本，而是沿短内路径估计测量条件下的干净均值，再将高斯源噪声直接输运到下一个噪声状态；借助去噪器兼容的充分统计量与协方差缩放的算子更新来完成条件均值估计。

Result: 在四个逆问题上的实验表明，DCTS达到了与竞争方法相当的重建质量，同时实现最高16.8倍的加速。

Conclusion: DCTS以更高效的方式实现了独立后验刷新的全局校正能力，在免训练扩散逆求解中兼顾了重建质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Training-free diffusion inverse solvers typically choose between local measurement guidance and costly clean-space posterior updates. Independent posterior refresh can improve global correction by sampling a clean conditional and re-noising it, but its practical realization requires probability-flow ODE integration and clean-space Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). We propose Direct Conditional Transition Sampling (DCTS), a direct stochastic-flow approximation to the same ideal refresh target. Rather than explicitly drawing a clean sample, DCTS estimates the measurement-conditioned clean mean along a short inner path and transports Gaussian source noise directly to the next noisy state. A denoiser-compatible sufficient statistic and a covariance-scaled operator update enable this conditional-mean estimation. Experiments on four inverse problems demonstrate that DCTS achieves competitive reconstruction quality with up to $16.8\times$ speedups over competing methods.

</details>


### [113] [Open-UniMo: Towards Unified Motion-Language Understanding and Generation in the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14615)
*Guocun Wang,Kenkun Liu,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Zhe Huang,Luyuan Zhang,Dake Zhong,Choo Sin Wai,Xiaoguang Han,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Open-UniMo统一大型运动-语言模型，通过共享token空间、运动一致性思维链与两阶段训练，实现运动生成与理解的双向统一，并配套Open-MoBench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-语言模型将运动视为语言的辅助模态，导致文本主导的表征与有限的跨模态交互；同时下一token预测范式不适合长运动序列，自回归生成会累积预测误差。

Method: 在Qwen约150K文本词表基础上扩展64K运动token，构建统一token空间实现模态对等；引入运动一致性思维链作为中间表征连接语言语义与运动动态；采用两阶段训练：监督微调建立CoT引导的双向映射，再用GRPO强化语义对齐并缓解自回归误差累积。

Result: 在传统指标与所提出的Open-MoBench（VLM引导，评估T2M生成、M2T理解及双向一致性）上均取得最优性能。消融表明M2T理解并非主要受限于运动token词表规模，将M2T与可学习的T2M生成路径耦合能获得更强的跨模态表征。

Conclusion: 在基于自回归的运动-语言建模中，生成能够促进理解，为统一的具身运动-语言建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unified motion generation and understanding is crucial for embodied AI systems that can both synthesize and interpret human actions in open-world environments. Existing motion-language models often treat motion as an auxiliary modality of a language model, leading to text-dominated representations and limited cross-modal interaction. Moreover, the next-token prediction paradigm is not naturally suited to long motion sequences, where autoregressive generation may accumulate prediction errors. To address these challenges, we propose Open-UniMo, a unified Large Motion-Language Model (LMLM) trained on million-scale open-world motion-language data. Open-UniMo promotes modality parity by extending Qwen's vocabulary of about 150K text tokens with 64K motion tokens, enabling motion and language to share a unified token space. We further introduce motion-consistent Chain-of-Thought reasoning as an intermediate representation to bridge language semantics and motion dynamics. Open-UniMo is trained with a two-stage pipeline, where supervised fine-tuning establishes CoT-guided bidirectional motion-language mapping and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves semantic alignment while mitigating cumulative errors in autoregressive motion-token generation. To support comprehensive evaluation, we propose Open-MoBench, a VLM-guided benchmark for assessing text-to-motion (T2M) generation, motion-to-text (M2T) understanding, and bidirectional consistency. Extensive experiments show that Open-UniMo achieves state-of-the-art performance on both conventional metrics and Open-MoBench. Furthermore, ablation studies reveal that M2T understanding is not primarily limited by motion-token vocabulary size; instead, coupling M2T with the learnable T2M generation path yields stronger cross-modal representations, demonstrating that generation can facilitate understanding in AR-based motion-language modeling.

</details>


### [114] [ESAFusion: LiDAR--4-D Radar Fusion via Local Geometric Complementation and Multiscale Adaptive Interaction for 3-D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14619)
*Gang Ma,Senjie Hu,Junjie Liu,Chao Wang,Hui Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ESAFusion 框架，融合 LiDAR 与 4-D 雷达实现 3-D 目标检测，通过证据感知雷达筛选、柱级互补编码和尺度自适应融合提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 与 4-D 雷达融合可结合精确空间几何与运动、反射率线索，适合复杂驾驶场景的 3-D 检测，但雷达观测稀疏、两模态空间采样不一致，且不同空间区域中模态与特征尺度的相对重要性不同，导致可靠互补与自适应融合困难。

Method: ESAFusion 包含三个模块：证据感知雷达筛选（ERS）利用运动与观测质量证据抑制雷达杂波并保留前景置信度；柱级互补编码器（PCE）借助邻近 LiDAR 柱的局部几何支撑，在空间采样不匹配下增强跨模态互补；尺度内与尺度间自适应融合（ISAF）在 BEV 空间自适应调节不同模态与特征尺度的贡献。

Result: 在 View-of-Delft（VoD）数据集上取得对比方法中最高的 mAP，Entire Annotated Area 为 74.60%，Driving Corridor 为 88.89%；两个区域中 Cyclist 的 AP 均为最高，运行速度 19.23 FPS。在 VoD-Fog 上的评估表明在 LiDAR 观测逐步退化时仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ESAFusion 通过证据感知与尺度自适应设计有效解决了 LiDAR--4-D 雷达融合中的稀疏性与采样不匹配问题，在精度与鲁棒性上表现优异，源代码将公开。

Abstract: LiDAR--4-D radar fusion combines accurate spatial geometry with motion and reflectivity cues from radar, offering a promising solution for 3-D object detection in complex driving environments. However, sparse radar observations and differences in spatial sampling between the two modalities complicate reliable cross-modal complementation. Moreover, the relative importance of modalities and feature scales varies across spatial regions, making adaptive fusion challenging. To address these challenges, we propose ESAFusion, an evidence-aware and scale-adaptive framework that combines local geometric complementation with multiscale adaptive interaction. Specifically, we introduce an Evidence-Aware Radar Selection (ERS) module to suppress radar clutter using motion and observation-quality evidence while retaining foreground confidence for subsequent fusion. Then, the Pillar-Level Complementary Encoder (PCE) improves cross-modal complementation under mismatched spatial sampling using local geometric support from neighboring LiDAR pillars. We further design an Intra- and Inter-Scale Adaptive Fusion (ISAF) module to adaptively adjust the contributions of different modalities and feature scales in bird's-eye-view (BEV) space. Extensive experiments on the View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset show that ESAFusion achieves the highest mean average precision (mAP) among the compared methods, reaching 74.60% in the Entire Annotated Area and 88.89% in the Driving Corridor. It also attains the highest average precision (AP) for Cyclist among these methods in both regions while running at 19.23 FPS. Evaluations on VoD-Fog further demonstrate robustness under progressively degraded LiDAR observations. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/SenJieHu549/ESAFusion.

</details>


### [115] [TTDF: A Two-Stage Framework for Reliable Surgical Phase Transition Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14624)
*Yushi Guo,Pietro Valdastri,Duygu Sarikaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段过渡检测框架TTDF，将可靠的工作流过渡检测定义为独立的事件级任务，在冻结的在线阶段识别器输出上逐步过滤过渡候选，在Cholec80上减少了误报过渡并保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线手术阶段识别器主要关注逐帧准确率和时间一致性，而非工作流过渡事件的可靠性。直接将阶段变化转为事件不可靠：时间抖动和工作流非法切换会产生误报或重复事件，而持续且符合工作流的候选也可能错误。因此需要将可靠的工作流过渡检测作为独立的事件级任务来研究。

Method: 提出两阶段过渡检测框架TTDF，一个因果框架，逐步过滤过渡候选。第一阶段过渡候选提取（TCE）应用最短持续时间要求和工作流图约束，去除由时间抖动和工作流图不允许的阶段转换引起的误报候选：仅当预测目标阶段持续最短时间且有序阶段对属于工作流图允许的过渡集时才保留候选，从而在无需额外训练的情况下产生高召回候选集。第二阶段过渡候选验证（TCV）利用冻结DINOv2特征中的阶段后验偏移和视觉变化线索抑制剩余误报候选。事件使用阶段对感知的一对一匹配协议进行评估。

Result: 在Cholec80上的实验表明，TTDF在保持召回率并控制决策延迟的同时，减少了误报过渡的发出。

Conclusion: TTDF通过事件级的两阶段因果过滤，有效提升了工作流过渡检测的可靠性，为上下文感知手术辅助和下游决策支持提供了更可信的过渡事件。

Abstract: Reliable workflow transition detection is important for context-aware surgical assistance and downstream decision support. However, online surgical phase recognizers primarily focus on frame-wise accuracy and temporal consistency, rather than the reliability of workflow transition events. Directly converting phase changes into events is unreliable: temporal jitter and workflow-illegal switches produce false or duplicate events, while persistent, workflow-consistent candidates may remain incorrect. To address this limitation, we formulate reliable workflow transition detection as a distinct event-level task operating on outputs of a frozen online phase recognizer. We propose the Two-Stage Transition Detection Framework (TTDF), a causal framework that progressively filters transition candidates. Transition Candidate Extraction (TCE) first applies a minimum-duration requirement and a workflow-graph constraint to remove false candidates caused by temporal jitter and phase transitions not allowed by the workflow graph. Specifically, a candidate is retained only if the predicted target phase persists for a minimum duration and the ordered phase pair belongs to the workflow graph's allowed transition set. TCE thereby produces a high-recall candidate set without additional training. Transition Candidate Verification (TCV) suppresses remaining false candidates using phase-posterior shifts and visual-change cues from frozen DINOv2 features. Events are assessed using a phase-pair-aware one-to-one matching protocol. Experiments on Cholec80 show that TTDF reduces false transition emissions while preserving recall and controlling decision delay.

</details>


### [116] [SCOUT-SLAM: Structurally-Coupled Dual Uncertainty-Aware 3DGS SLAM in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14634)
*Kumaran Karthik,Pramat Shastri Jois,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOUT-SLAM，一个结构耦合的双不确定性框架，通过共享基础网络同时估计跟踪不确定性与重建不确定性，缓解了 3DGS-SLAM 中相机跟踪精度与重建质量之间的循环依赖问题，在动态场景基准上实现了最优的跟踪精度与无伪影静态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS-SLAM 方法在快速相机运动和杂乱动态环境中，依赖底层场景重建的稳定性来建模不确定性，导致相机跟踪精度与重建质量之间形成循环依赖：重建不稳定会削弱不确定性建模，进而影响跟踪精度与静态场景重建。作者希望打破这一循环依赖。

Method: 提出 SCOUT-SLAM，采用结构耦合的双不确定性框架。两种不确定性均由一个共享基础网络估计；在此基础上，通过基于多视角特征一致性训练的低秩适配（LoRA）分支估计不单纯依赖重建质量的跟踪不确定性；同时引入空间自适应先验来调制网络训练目标，使重建不稳定不会在静态区域上抬高不确定性，从而为两个分支保持共享表示的完整性。

Result: 在动态场景基准（TUM RGB-D、Bonn Dynamic、Wild-SLAM MoCap）上的评估表明，SCOUT-SLAM 达到了当前最优的相机跟踪精度，并实现了无伪影的静态场景重建。

Conclusion: 通过结构耦合的双不确定性设计与多视角一致性驱动的低秩适配，SCOUT-SLAM 有效解耦了跟踪不确定性与重建质量，缓解了循环依赖问题，在动态环境下同时提升了跟踪精度与静态重建质量，为 3DGS-SLAM 在复杂真实场景中的鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM (3DGS-SLAM) has gained significant momentum in simultaneous localization and 3DGS scene reconstruction. In real-world scenarios with rapid camera motion and cluttered dynamic environments, existing methods rely on the stability of the underlying scene reconstruction to model uncertainty. This leads to a circular dependency between camera tracking accuracy and reconstruction quality: reconstruction instabilities degrade uncertainty modeling, which affects accurate camera tracking and static scene reconstruction. To address this, the paper proposes SCOUT-SLAM, a structurally-coupled dual-uncertainty framework in which both uncertainties are estimated from a shared base network. A low-rank adaptation of this network, trained on multi-view feature consistency, estimates a tracking uncertainty that does not depend solely on the reconstruction quality. A spatially-adaptive prior modulates the network's training objective so that reconstruction instability does not inflate uncertainty on static regions, keeping the shared representation intact for both branches. Evaluations on dynamic benchmarks (TUM RGB-D, Bonn Dynamic, Wild-SLAM MoCap) demonstrate that SCOUT-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art camera tracking accuracy and artifact-free static scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [117] [Evaluating Contextual Bias in CNN Image Classification: Evidence from Agricultural Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14654)
*Abhilekha Dalal,Michael Okonoda,Eder Martinez,Lior Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统检验了CNN农业图像分类中模型是否依赖背景语境信息，发现八个数据集中有六个的背景主导区块分类准确率高于随机水平，且语境依赖的成因（类别不平衡 vs 真实类别判别信息）因数据集和架构而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN通常用留存分类准确率评估，但这预设预测主要基于目标物体本身而非周围语境。作者希望检验这一假设在农业图像分类中是否成立，即模型是否利用了背景语境信息作出预测。

Method: 在八个公开农业基准数据集和四种常用CNN架构上，对比原始图像与从同一图像中提取的背景主导区块的分类性能；建立数据集特定的随机概率基线并进行语境偏差分类。对表现显著高于随机的四个数据集，进一步用宏平均精确率、召回率和F1以及类别平衡测试子集，区分语境依赖是源于真实类别判别信息还是类别不平衡。

Result: 八个数据集中有六个的背景主导区块分类准确率高于数据集特定随机水平，其中四个显著高于随机，表明语境信息对多数数据集的模型预测有贡献。对这四个数据集的进一步分析显示，语境依赖没有单一解释：部分数据集和架构的观测信号大部分由类别不平衡造成，而另一些在平衡后仍保持高于随机的语境分类能力。

Conclusion: 语境偏差是CNN图像分类中反复出现的特征，而非局限于单一应用领域的现象，与先前在物体识别和癌症病理成像中的证据一致。该工作提供了一个系统框架，通过结合数据集特定随机基线、语境偏差分类、宏平均评估和类别平衡稳健性分析，量化异构图像数据集中的语境偏差。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are typically evaluated using held-out classification accuracy, an approach that presupposes predictions are based primarily on the intended object of interest rather than incidental surrounding context. We test this assumption in CNN-based agricultural image classification by comparing model performance on original images with performance on background-dominated patches extracted from the same images across eight publicly available agricultural benchmark datasets and four widely used CNN architectures. Background-dominated patches were classified above dataset-specific random chance for six of the eight datasets, and substantially above chance for four of them, indicating that contextual information contributes to model predictions for the majority of datasets evaluated. For these four datasets, we further evaluated whether this behavior reflected genuine class-discriminative information or was primarily attributable to class imbalance using macro-averaged precision, recall, and F1 together with class-balanced test subsets. The results show that contextual reliance does not admit a single explanation: class imbalance accounts for a substantial portion of the observed signal for some datasets and architectures, whereas above-chance contextual classification persists after balancing for others. Together with previous evidence from curated object recognition and cancer pathology imaging, these findings support the growing view that contextual bias is a recurring characteristic of CNN-based image classification rather than a phenomenon confined to a single application domain. More broadly, this work provides a systematic framework for quantifying contextual bias across heterogeneous image datasets by combining dataset-specific random-chance baselines, contextual bias categorization, macro-averaged evaluation, and class-balanced robustness analysis.

</details>


### [118] [Compositional SVG Generation via VLM-Driven Hierarchical Semantic Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14657)
*Sehwan Park,Taehoon Kim,Geonhee Han,Dohyun Kim,Seung Wook Kim,Paul Hongsuck Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLM驱动的智能体框架，实现语义组合式SVG生成，并构建新基准评估结构组合性与可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVG生成流程多产出扁平、语义无感知的路径集合，编辑单个对象需手动识别其组成路径，缺乏结构化与可编辑性。

Method: 基于VLM的智能体框架，通过自顶向下分解、视觉定位和提示驱动的非模态遮挡恢复，递归解析视觉场景为语义与几何层次结构；同时提出Semantic SVG Benchmark及语义召回/精确率、PERE等子组件指标。

Result: 原生预测的结构在分组质量和可编辑性上超越现有扁平生成方法的上限，同时保持最先进的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 语义组合式SVG生成方法有效提升了结构化程度与功能可编辑性，为可编辑矢量图形生成提供了新范式与评估标准。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at visual reasoning, generating structured, editable Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) remains a fundamental challenge. Existing pipelines predominantly yield flat, semantically agnostic collections of paths, where editing a single object requires manually identifying its constituent paths. To address this, we propose a VLM-driven agentic framework for semantic compositional SVG generation. Our pipeline recursively parses visual scenes into semantic and geometric hierarchies via top-down decomposition, visual grounding, and prompt-driven amodal occlusion recovery, ensuring each component is geometrically complete. Furthermore, we introduce the Semantic SVG Benchmark with human-annotated semantic groups and novel sub-component metrics (Semantic Recall/Precision, PERE) to explicitly evaluate structural compositionality and functional editability. Experiments show that our natively predicted structures surpass the upper bounds of existing flat-generation methods in both grouping quality and editability, while maintaining state-of-the-art visual fidelity.

</details>


### [119] [Floquet Fibre Geometry and Higher-Order Reduced Coordinates for Off-Manifold Transients near Nonlinear Aeroelastic Flutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14674)
*Puxue Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究吸引极限环附近降维坐标的纤维几何正确性，证明一阶相位-等稳定图必须沿强稳定商纤维投影，并在非线性气动弹性极限环上验证度量正交投影会引入O(delta)残差，而学习的更高阶修正因诊断依赖表示而无法确立独立结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在吸引极限环附近给状态赋予约化坐标，需要正确的不变纤维几何。经典的一阶相位-等稳定图由伴随Floquet模得到，沿强稳定商纤维投影；而保留慢束的度量正交补一般并非不变纤维。作者希望检验这种几何差异对残差标度的影响，并评估学习到的更高阶修正是否真正提供额外预测价值。

Method: 局部证明满足线性化半共轭关系的图会留下O(delta^2)不变性残差，而沿非不变补投影一般留下O(delta)项。在非线性气动弹性极限环上比较度量法向与强稳定方向的角度差，并用无拟合参数方式替换纤维以测量残差标度变化。进一步测试线性化被固定到伴随Floquet图、对称性精确且约化流固定的学习高阶修正。

Result: 非线性气动弹性极限环中，度量法向与强稳定方向相差48.5至71.7度，度量法向扰动含一阶保留相位与慢幅值分量。用强稳定纤维替换度量法向后，测得残差标度从delta^1.01变为delta^1.87，无需拟合参数。学习的高阶修正虽降低了固定归一化潜残差，但事后幅值重标定与面向伴随Floquet目标的前向一致性会移动甚至反转排序。

Conclusion: 由于学习映射已与基线共享一阶规范，且前向目标由基线图提供，这些诊断既不能确立独立的正面也不能确立负面的更高阶结果。在该基准中正确的一阶Floquet几何是必要的，而学习修正的额外预测价值仍未被现有依赖表示的诊断所识别。

Abstract: Assigning reduced coordinates to states near an attracting limit cycle requires the correct invariant-fibre geometry. The classical first-order phase-isostable chart obtained from adjoint Floquet modes projects along the strong-stable quotient fibre, whereas a metric-orthogonal complement of the retained slow bundle generally does not. We prove locally that a chart satisfying the linearised semiconjugacy relation leaves an O(delta^2) invariance residual, while projection along a non-invariant complement generically leaves an O(delta) term. For a nonlinear aeroelastic limit cycle, the metric-normal and strong-stable directions differ by 48.5 to 71.7 degrees, and metric-normal perturbations contain first-order retained phase and slow-amplitude components. Replacing the metric normal by the strong-stable fibre changes the measured residual scaling from delta^1.01 to delta^1.87 without fitted parameters. We then test learned higher-order corrections whose linearisation is pinned to the adjoint-Floquet chart, whose symmetry is exact, and whose reduced flow is fixed. Although they reduce the registered fixed-normalisation latent residual, post-hoc amplitude recalibration and adjoint-Floquet-targeted future consistency move or reverse the ranking. Because the learned maps already share the baseline's first-order gauge and the future target is supplied by the baseline chart, these diagnostics establish neither an independent positive nor negative higher-order result. Correct first-order Floquet geometry is therefore necessary in this benchmark, while the additional predictive value of the learned correction remains unidentified by the available representation-dependent diagnostics.

</details>


### [120] [From Density to Biopsy Decisions and Malignancy Prediction: A Benchmark Study of Multimodal Large Language Models Against Radiologists in Digital and Contrast-Enhanced Mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14676)
*Ali Abbasian Ardakani,Afshin Mohammadi,Taha Yusuf Kuzan,Beyza Nur Kuzan,Alisa Mohebbi,Masume Behruzi,Hamid Khorshidi,Ashkan Ghorbani,Elham Asadiara,Zeinab Khorshidi Lotfi,Ansar Rahman,Nedim Christoph Beste,U. Rajendra Acharya,Sepideh Hatamikia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究对比了四种多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）与不同经验水平放射科医生在乳腺密度评估、BI-RADS分级、活检指征判定及恶性概率连续估计中的表现，发现放射科医生在分类任务上普遍优于MLLMs，但经过病灶掩膜处理的MLLMs在恶性概率连续估计上接近人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多模态大语言模型在乳腺影像（数字乳腺摄影DM与对比增强乳腺摄影CEM）临床决策任务中的能力，明确其相对放射科医生在不同任务类型上的差距，并探索病灶掩膜等辅助手段能否提升模型表现，从而判断MLLMs在临床中的潜在辅助价值。

Method: 纳入179名具有配对DM/CEM检查及参考标准的女性。四种MLLMs（ChatGPT-5.2、Gemini-3.1 Pro、Sonnet-4.6、Muse Spark）分别在有掩膜和无掩膜条件下解读图像；三位放射科医生仅解读无掩膜图像。评估任务包括二分类乳腺密度、五分类BI-RADS、二分类活检指征及连续恶性概率估计。

Result: DM二分类密度：放射科医生准确率55.81%–78.60%，多数高于MLLMs（62.33%–71.63%），掩膜增益有限。五分类BI-RADS：放射科医生DM 56.74%–67.44%、CEM 62.33%–82.79%，高于MLLMs（DM 31.16%–45.12%；CEM 40.00%–55.81%）。二分类活检指征：放射科医生DM 85.12%–89.77%、CEM 86.98%–92.09%，高于MLLMs（DM 61.39%–75.35%；CEM 69.30%–82.79%），CEM对所有读者均有提升。病灶掩膜显著提升MLLM恶性概率估计准确率：DM由64.65%–71.63%升至72.56%–78.60%，CEM由67.91%–77.21%升至72.56%–81.86%，接近放射科医生范围（DM 63.72%–82.79%；CEM 81.86%–88.84%），顶级掩膜模型的AUC与人类读者重叠。MLLMs中Muse Spark表现最佳，其次为Sonnet-4.6。

Conclusion: 放射科医生在分类任务上总体优于MLLMs；经掩膜处理的选定模型在恶性概率连续估计上接近人类表现，提示MLLMs可能作为临床辅助工具发挥作用。

Abstract: Purpose: To compare four multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with radiologists of varying expertise in breast density assessment, BI-RADS assessment, biopsy candidacy determination, and continuous malignancy probability estimation using digital mammography (DM) and contrast-enhanced mammography (CEM). Methods: This study included 179 women with paired DM/CEM examinations and reference standards. Four MLLMs (ChatGPT-5.2, Gemini-3.1 Pro, Sonnet-4.6, Muse Spark) interpreted images with and without masks; three radiologists interpreted non-masked images. Results: For binary density classification on DM, radiologist accuracies ranged from 55.81% to 78.60%, exceeding most MLLM values (62.33%-71.63%), while masks added limited benefit. Five-category BI-RADS accuracies were higher for radiologists on DM (56.74-67.44%) and CEM (62.33-82.79%) compared with MLLMs (DM 31.16-45.12%; CEM 40.00-55.81%). Binary biopsy-candidacy accuracies were likewise higher for radiologists (DM 85.12-89.77%; CEM 86.98-92.09%) than for MLLMs (DM 61.39-75.35%; CEM 69.30-82.79%), although CEM improved performance across all readers. Lesion masks substantially improved MLLM continuous malignancy-probability accuracies from 64.65%-71.63% to 72.56-78.60% on DM and from 67.91%-77.21% to 72.56%-81.86% on CEM, approaching radiologist ranges (DM 63.72-82.79%; CEM 81.86-88.84%). The corresponding AUCs for the top masked models overlapped those of the human readers. Overall, Muse Spark, followed by Sonnet-4.6, demonstrated the strongest performance among the MLLMs across domains. Conclusion: Radiologists generally outperformed MLLMs in categorical tasks, while selected masked models approached human performance for continuous malignancy probability estimation, suggesting a potential adjunctive role.

</details>


### [121] [LIMODENet: Attention-Free Compact Encoders for Information-Preserving Onboard Satellite Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14690)
*Thanh-Dung Le,Vu Nguyen Ha,Ti Ti Nguyen,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIMODENet（LinearMix-ODENet），一个 0.69M 参数、无 softmax 与 QKV 的编码器骨干，可在神经形态加速器上端到端转换为脉冲网络，在 DVB-S2X 退化图像恢复中优于同参数量 CNN 自编码器与 U-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载卫星需在数瓦功耗下恢复信道退化图像，而 BrainChip Akida、Intel Loihi-2 等神经形态加速器不支持 softmax 或注意力操作。因此需要研究在此约束下哪种编码器恢复效果最好。

Method: 提出 LIMODENet（LinearMix-ODENet）：0.69M 参数、无 softmax/QKV 的骨干网络；其残差阶段可解读为 ODE 离散化，并经验性地保持信息（探针准确率从 stem 到 head 由 79.9% 升至 98.4%）。

Result: 在同参数量下，LIMODENet 对 DVB-S2X 退化的 EuroSAT 恢复效果优于 CNN 自编码器（+1.75 dB PSNR）和带跳连的 U-Net（+1.07 dB），三个随机种子结果不重叠。无约束的现代恢复器（NAFNet、Restormer）在保真度上更优；差距分解显示：脉冲合法的加性跳连可挽回约一半差距，其余源于注意力与通道门控。LIMODENet 可端到端转换为脉冲网络，零被阻塞操作，而对比方法为 22-24 个。

Conclusion: LIMODENet 并非目前最好的恢复器，但在真实功耗预算下是最佳的可验证可部署方案。

Abstract: Onboard satellites must restore a channel-degraded image on a few watts, using neuromorphic accelerators (e.g., BrainChip Akida, Intel Loihi-2) that support no softmax or attention. We ask which encoder restores best under that constraint and introduce LIMODENet (LinearMix-ODENet), a 0.69M softmax-/QKV-free backbone whose residual stages read as ODE discretizations and which is empirically information-preserving (probe accuracy rises 79.9% -> 98.4% from stem to head). At iso-parameters it restores 1 dB DVB-S2X-degraded EuroSAT better than a CNN autoencoder (+1.75 dB PSNR) and a skip-connection U-Net (+1.07 dB), three seeds, non-overlapping. Unconstrained modern restorers (NAFNet, Restormer) win on fidelity; we decompose that gap: spiking-legal additive skips recover about half, and the rest traces to attention and channel gating. LIMODENet then converts end-to-end to a spiking network with zero blocked operations, versus 22-24 for the competitors: not the best restorer available, but the best verified deployable within a real power budget.

</details>


### [122] [Perceive, Refine, Reason: A Calibrated Pipeline for Measuring Indicators in Strategic Visual Communication on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14699)
*Weihong Qi,Chen Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"感知—精化—推理"(PRR)校准流程，将视觉语言检测器转化为可审计的社会科学研究测量工具，在103,920张Facebook图片上揭示保守派议员展示更大美国国旗的视觉模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体视觉内容影响受众认知，但现有图像分析工具依赖预定义类别或仅给出粗糙的图像级标签，难以规模化测量具体对象是否出现、显著程度及空间位置，形成测量缺口。

Method: PRR结合自然语言类别提示、基于SAM的像素级空间精化，以及多模态LLM推理仲裁层（其推理链外化领域知识、降低人工校验门槛）；配套三层可审计框架，运用定量学习刻画各类别可靠性、支持任务对齐配置并统计校正流行度估计。在4个视觉语言检测器和9个社会学类别上验证。

Result: 相比零样本基线取得显著精度提升，最强主干提升43.3个百分点。应用于2024选举周期美国议员发布的103,920张Facebook图片并结合DW-NOMINATE意识形态得分，发现更保守议员将美国国旗展示为更大的视觉元素，边缘化摆放倾向较弱——这是二值检测无法发现的空间模式。

Conclusion: PRR为计算社会科学家提供了模型无关、易用、空间落地且可校正的视觉测量工具箱。

Abstract: Visual content shapes audience perception and opinion on social media, and computational social science increasingly relies on automated tools to analyze images at scale. Yet a measurement gap persists: existing tools rely on predefined categories or produce only coarse image-level labels, while measuring which specific objects appear in an image, how prominently, and where in the frame remains difficult at scale. We introduce Perceive, Refine, Reason (PRR), a calibrated pipeline that turns flexible vision-language detectors into auditable measurement instruments for social-scientific research. PRR combines natural-language category prompts with pixel-level spatial refinement via the Segment Anything Model (SAM) and a multimodal LLM arbitration layer whose reasoning chains externalize domain knowledge and lower the expertise threshold for human-in-the-loop validation. A complementary three-tier auditability framework applies quantification learning to profile per-category reliability, support task-aligned configuration, and statistically correct prevalence estimates. Across four vision-language detectors and nine sociological categories, the pipeline yields substantial precision gains over zero-shot baselines, including a 43.3-point improvement for the strongest backbone. Applying PRR to 103,920 Facebook images from U.S. legislators during the 2024 election cycle and linking detections to DW-NOMINATE ideology scores, we find that more conservative legislators display U.S. flags as larger visual elements, with a weaker tendency toward peripheral placement, a spatial pattern invisible to binary detection. PRR provides computational social scientists with a model-agnostic toolkit for accessible, spatially-grounded, and correctable visual measurement.

</details>


### [123] [CrossDistill: Balancing Quality and Diversity via Trajectory-Level Hybrid Few-Step Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14725)
*Yuxi Liu,Haoyu Li,Yixiang Cai,Tengxu Sun,Zekun Zhang,Baole Ai,Ang Wang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Kun Yuan,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossDistill 提出一种沿噪声轴划分轨迹的混合蒸馏框架，在高噪声区间保留轨迹、低噪声区间做分布匹配，从而同时兼顾少步生成的多样性与保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏需要在多样性与保真度之间权衡：基于轨迹的蒸馏能保留模式覆盖，而分布匹配虽能提升样本质量却会损失多样性。作者发现该矛盾可按噪声区间分解，高噪声步决定全局模式，低噪声步精修局部细节，因而存在可被利用的结构性张力。

Method: 提出 CrossDistill，一种轨迹级混合蒸馏框架。它在交叉点处切分采样轨迹：高噪声区间施加轨迹保持目标，低噪声区间施加分布匹配目标，并通过交叉状态耦合两阶段。与损失级混合不同，也补充训练期两阶段方案，它在噪声轴上显式分配互补目标。该方法是噪声级调度策略，PCM 与 DMD 为可插拔实例，关键设计在于噪声划分、交叉耦合与目标顺序。

Result: 在文本到视频扩散模型上的实验及图像到视频的定性结果显示，CrossDistill 拓展了少步生成的质量—多样性前沿，在保持种子级变化的同时取得有竞争力的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 沿噪声轴显式分配互补蒸馏目标是一种有效策略，能在少步扩散蒸馏中缓解多样性与保真度的冲突，且作为噪声级调度策略具有良好的可插拔性与扩展性。

Abstract: Few-step distillation accelerates diffusion models but must balance diversity and fidelity: trajectory-based distillation preserves mode coverage, while distribution matching sharpens samples but can reduce diversity. We show that this tension can be exploited in a noise-regime-dependent way: high-noise steps largely determine global modes, whereas low-noise steps refine local details. We propose CrossDistill, a trajectory-level hybrid distillation framework that splits the sampling trajectory at a crossover point, applies a trajectory-preserving objective on the high-noise interval and a distribution-matching objective on the low-noise interval, and couples the two stages through the crossover state. In contrast to loss-level mixing, and complementarily to training-time two-stage recipes, CrossDistill explicitly assigns complementary objectives along the noise axis, so that global branching is preserved before local statistics are sharpened. CrossDistill is a noise-level scheduling policy: PCM and DMD are plug-in instantiations, while the noise partition, crossover coupling, and objective ordering are the key design elements. Experiments on text-to-video diffusion models and qualitative image-to-video results show that CrossDistill expands the few-step quality-diversity frontier, retaining seed-level variation while achieving competitive visual fidelity.

</details>


### [124] [Occlusal Geometry in Closed Form for Orthodontic Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13237)
*Ajo Babu George,Govind Arun,Sidharth N Krishna,Uma Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用Bite2Text扫描已配准于咬合的特性，直接从几何量测生成正畸报告，而非依赖多模态推断；融合影像分类后字段准确率由0.601提升至0.683，在ODIN 2026 Bite2Text测试阶段获第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正畸报告生成通常被视为多模态图像描述任务，但Bite2Text发布的扫描对已处于咬合配准状态，使得覆咬合、覆盖等核心咬合量可直接测量而非推断。作者希望充分利用这一数据属性，构建可解释、可复现的确定性生成流程，并揭示现有评测指标与临床一致性方面存在的问题。

Method: 方法上，作者放弃数据说明中的RAS坐标系约定，改由牙弓锥度与牙弓闭合度恢复解剖坐标系；将每个牙弓简化为牙弓角坐标下的咬合嵴轮廓，解析求解覆咬合、覆盖、中线偏斜、横向重叠、反咬合范围、牙尖交错滞后及咬合曲线等量。随后用梯度提升将31个测量量映射到13个模板字段，仅当患者级交叉验证优于其多数类基线时才对该字段进行预测，并由确定性渲染器生成六段式叙述。同时用ConvNeXt-Tiny对五张标准化照片视图分类，按字段融合以提升准确率。

Result: 字段融合使平均字段准确率从0.601升至0.683。对挑战评测器的复现表明其BLEU-4与METEOR为局部变体，F均值权重中召回占九成；两名临床医生对同一患者的发现一致率仅47%，且恒定报告在描述指标上高出真实第二位临床医生报告0.165。留出集上对口内扫描参考的BLEU-4为0.458、METEOR为0.677，对照片参考为0.278与0.507；提交系统在ODIN 2026 Bite2Text测试阶段位列第三（0.2680与0.4629），距第一名BLEU-4差0.022，CPU上每例运行不足十秒。

Conclusion: 本研究说明，在已配准的咬合扫描上，正畸报告可主要通过几何测量与确定性规则生成，无需复杂推断。同时指出当前描述类评测指标对召回过度加权并与临床一致性脱节，提示Bite2Text类任务的评测设计需要改进。数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Orthodontic report generation from intraoral data is normally cast as multimodal captioning, yet the released Bite2Text scan pairs are supplied already registered in occlusion, which makes several core occlusal quantities directly measurable rather than inferable. The system reported here exploits that property: an anatomical frame is recovered per case from arch taper and arch closure instead of the stated RAS convention, which does not hold across the release, and each arch is reduced to an occlusal ridge profile in arch-angle coordinates yielding overbite, overjet, midline deviation, transverse overlap, crossbite extent, cusp interdigitation lag, and the occlusal curves in closed form. Gradient boosting maps 31 such measurements onto 13 template fields, a field being predicted only where patient-level cross-validation beats its own majority baseline, and a deterministic renderer emits the corpus six-part narrative; a ConvNeXt-Tiny classifier over the five standardised photographic views is fused per field, raising mean field accuracy from 0.601 to 0.683. Reimplementation of the challenge evaluator shows that its BLEU-4 and METEOR are local variants whose F-mean weights recall nine to one, that two clinicians agree on 47 percent of findings for the same patient, and that a constant report consequently outscores a genuine second clinician report by 0.165 captioning. Held-out scores reach BLEU-4 0.458 and METEOR 0.677 against intraoral scan references and 0.278 and 0.507 against photograph references, and the submitted system placed third in the ODIN 2026 Bite2Text test phase at 0.2680 and 0.4629, within 0.022 BLEU-4 of first, running on CPU in under ten seconds per case. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/GIND123/ODIN_toothfairy4

</details>


### [125] [From Visual Feedback to Textual Reviews: A Multi-Agent Vision-Language Framework for Image-Grounded Review Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14761)
*Utsav Kumar Nareti,Ayush Bansal,Kumari Priya,Chandranath Adak,Soumi Chattopadhyay,Muhammad Saqib,Saeed Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"图像驱动的评论辅助"新任务：从用户上传的商品图片生成可编辑的评论草稿，并采用多智能体视觉-语言框架实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台中用户上传的图片/视频反馈日益普遍，但视觉反馈缺乏情境解释与主观意见，而用户因撰写成本高往往文字反馈有限，二者之间存在信息缺口。

Method: 提出多智能体视觉-语言框架，包含四个专门角色：商品定位、视觉情感估计、视觉证据生成、评论合成；并采用显式中间表示（商品实体、预测评分、证据摘要）以增强可解释性与视觉基础。任务不同于传统图像描述，需商品级理解、情感估计与证据驱动的评论撰写，并应对图像质量差、过度放大、目标模糊、商品部分可见等真实场景挑战。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews Electronics数据集的精选子集上实验，验证了从视觉反馈生成连贯、商品感知、情感感知的评论草稿的可行性。

Conclusion: 这是首个将图像驱动评论辅助建模为多智能体视觉-语言推理问题的研究，为电商系统中的AI辅助评论撰写提供了实用的一步。

Abstract: Visual feedback in the form of user-uploaded images and videos is becoming increasingly common in e-commerce platforms because it provides authentic evidence of product quality, defects, packaging conditions, and real-world usage. However, visual feedback alone often lacks the contextual explanations and subjective opinions necessary for informed decision-making, while many users provide limited textual feedback due to the effort required to compose detailed reviews. To bridge this gap, we introduce image-grounded review assistance, a novel task that aims to generate editable review drafts from user-uploaded product images.
